17eda9e66d0d83688abed86a606f586579ad3d60
parent
505157f2d4b09c55408597b31864946b1d12ee91 [ diff ] 
[cbor] Simplify and expand CBOR Value fuzz domain

- Construct fuzztest::Domain<Value> directly instead of using an
  intermediate CborAST representation, improving fuzzer performance by
  avoiding constructing an intermediate AST tree and recursively copying
  and allocating a second cbor::Value tree on every iteration.
- Exercise bytestring map keys, booleans, null, undefined, full valid
  UTF-8 strings, and empty arrays/maps.
- Avoid unnecessary copies and string-to-vector conversions when loading
  the CBOR seed corpus by moving the std::string seeds out of
  fuzztest::ReadFilesFromDirectory, attaching .WithSeeds(GetCborCorpus)
  directly to the std::string domain (eliminating
  GetCborCorpusWithConfig), and viewing std::string inputs via
  base::as_byte_span right before parsing.
- Remove redundant format_cbor helper in favor of Value's ostream
  operator, extend the anonymous namespace, and update stale test name
  references in comments.

e7a7869d8d6fc91fc1218138885afea1da52483a
parent
09ac73beeda559f46a4aec68b8dbd5e93cf8c47f [ diff ] 
OpenXR: Avoid blocking WebXR frame pacing on browser overlay

When the browser overlay is visible during an active WebXR session:
- Defer RequestNextOverlayPose until SendFrameData has dispatched the
  frame to WebXR so the overlay does not invoke xrWaitFrame() ahead of
  WebXR and steal VSync frame pacing.
- Do not block WebXR composition on waiting_for_overlay_ when WebXR is
  visible; instead reuse the most recently submitted overlay texture so
  WebXR runs at full frame rate.
- Avoid rendering the redundant OpenXR base layer when WebXR uses a
  custom projection layer and the overlay is composited directly into
  that layer.

Screen recording: https://drive.google.com/file/d/1nBcjKhL2lXdGMn7zPJqGp5E7jTerHLb0/view?usp=sharing&resourcekey=0-KvjiSGK0FzSV--O4sXkfCQ

Test: See screen recording

[iOS] Use full-height choice screen learn more screen

With the SearchEngineChoiceScreenSnackbar feature enabled, the Learn
More screen text doesn't fit in the current `mediumDetent` height. In
order that the text is visible by default, while avoiding complex
client-side logic, use a full-height element.

[iOS] Fix Lottie animation color overrides for keypaths sharing suffixes

This CL fixes a bug where lottie animations' colors were ignored because
of a regex with a leading wildcard, grouping different keypaths sharing
the same suffix. To fix it keys are sorted by length so that shorter
keys are processed first.

[ios] Automatic conversion to TypedProfileKeyedServiceFactoryIOS

This CL converts most of the ProfileKeyerServiceFactoryIOS sub-classes
to the TypedProfileKeyedServiceFactoryIOS<Factory, Service> template,
which reduces the boilerplate by automatically generating the methods
GetForProfile(), GetForProfileIfExists() and GetInstance().

The conversion was automated using the script that can be found here:
https://paste.googleplex.com/5101783618289664

The script invocation was:

  $ git grep -l 'public ProfileKeyedServiceFactoryIOS'|\
    xargs python3 convert.py && git cl format

Only the files that were correctly migrated by the script were kept.
Any files whose migration failed or caused error was either modified
to be converted automatically without error or manually converted.

This CL was uploaded by git cl split.

R=ginnyhuang@chromium.org

[macOS] Don't launch app shims while the browser is shutting down
📋 Metadata
commit
a32d2fef92a773f3bd515b4b6a6afb04e4c3a05e
[ log ]
author

Thu Oct 15 17:06:09 2026
tree
ee7c73379ceae9eca1659fc7a113cd4f92d40315
parent
8f4e5324ac61fafa2ae3bdf7325be0bb0ac3b5f8 [ diff ] 
[macOS] Don't launch app shims while the browser is shutting down

Once the browser has started shutting down, the AppShimListener may
already have been destroyed (in BrowserProcessPlatformPart::
StartTearDown()), and Chrome will exit soon. An app shim launched at
that point will likely never connect. It instead keeps polling until
Chrome exits, and then crashes with "Running chrome instance terminated
before connecting." in AppShimController::PollForChromeReady().

With notification attribution, background app shim launches (for
NotificationDispatcherMojo and notification permission requests), as
well as AppShimHost's recreate-and-retry launch, can happen during
shutdown, which contributed to the PollForChromeReady crash spike.

Fail such launches in AppShimManager instead of launching a shim:
- LaunchShimInBackgroundMode() reports that no host is available, as it
  already does when no profile can be found.
- OnShimLaunchRequested() reports the launch as failed. This is done
  asynchronously, as it would be for a real launch, since it can be
  called synchronously from AppShimHost::LaunchShim(). For consistency,
  the existing failure cases in OnShimLaunchRequested() (no loaded
  profiles, app no longer installed) are now reported asynchronously as
  well.

📝 Commit Message (First Line)
[Mac] Always clear first responder before hiding WebContents NSViews

[Mac] Always clear first responder before hiding WebContents NSViews

This fixes an OOM error on fullscreen exit while the WebUI toolbar is
first responder before the exit. An existing old fix
(crbug.com/40261565) was already in WebContentsNSViewBridge::SetVisible
but it only handles WebContents in the main browser window
(NativeWidgetMacNSWindow), while the WebUI Toolbar adds WebContents in
NSToolbarFullscreenWindow, so the fix does not work.

This CL fixes the issue by universally applying the fix, regardless of
whether the WebContents is in main or fullscreen window.

Gated by kClearFirstResponderBeforeHidingWebContents kill switch.

9856ef6e7fcbbb8db351d6f1b185154d68ffd974 [ diff ] 
[ios] Automatic conversion to TypedProfileKeyedServiceFactoryIOS

This CL converts most of the ProfileKeyerServiceFactoryIOS sub-classes
to the TypedProfileKeyedServiceFactoryIOS<Factory, Service> template,
which reduces the boilerplate by automatically generating the methods
GetForProfile(), GetForProfileIfExists() and GetInstance().

The conversion was automated using the script that can be found here:
https://paste.googleplex.com/5101783618289664

The script invocation was:

  $ git grep -l 'public ProfileKeyedServiceFactoryIOS'|\
    xargs python3 convert.py && git cl format

Only the files that were correctly migrated by the script were kept.
Any files whose migration failed or caused error was either modified
to be converted automatically without error or manually converted.

This CL was uploaded by git cl split.

R=olivierrobin@chromium.org

parent
78e6822023fae7bb42babdc5cff6c7c347edfbdd [ diff ] 
[TabUI Virtualization] Add TabGroupItem and GroupedTabListDataProvider

Introduces TabGroupItem and the initial snapshot implementation of
GroupedTabListDataProvider to unify grouped tab projection across
collapsed-only (Grid Tab Switcher) and expandable (Vertical Tabs)
surfaces via a single supportsGroupExpansion configuration flag:
* TabGroupItem represents a tab group header or card item with immutable
  value semantics (group Token, selection state, and collapse state).
* GroupedTabListDataProvider projects contiguous grouped tabs in
  TabModel into a TabGroupItem, followed by child TabItems when
  supportsGroupExpansion is enabled and the group is expanded.
* In collapsed-only mode (supportsGroupExpansion=false), groups are
  always treated as collapsed and selection of a grouped tab highlights
  the parent TabGroupItem; in expandable mode
  (supportsGroupExpansion=true), selection highlights the child TabItem
  row.

[SettingsRefresh2026] Base styling for settings rows

This CL applies some new row styling rules for settings refresh 2026.
When styling other pages for this feature flag we will be using these
styling rules as a base.

[remoting] Forward per-terminal AgentStatus to the client

Add the TerminalControl::AgentInfo protocol buffer message to report the
AI coding agent state (AgentState enum) and an optional conversation
title for a terminal session.

Add AgentState and AgentStatus (mapped to
protocol::TerminalControl::AgentInfo across Mojo) to common.mojom and
add a SetAgentStatus method to ChromotingSessionServices.

PeerSessionImpl implements ChromotingSessionServices::SetAgentStatus,
verifies that the terminal ID corresponds to an active session in
TerminalSessionManager, and delivers a TerminalControl::AgentInfo
message to the client.

The production caller (SendAgentStatusMain in host_main.cc) is
implemented in the stacked child CL (https://crrev.com/c/8546813).

e783f43c8c43cef27ca56bfdbfa9ee18c5edacfc

tree
5436cfdf6afa9878e16177ee9e6cbf1fafe68e4e
parent
df4c9900cfa13a214bd6e78d72302c1b7a706527 [ diff ] 
[A11y] Serialize zero font size for text formatting

A text node with font size 0 is valid and makes text invisible, but
Blink only serialized ax::mojom::FloatAttribute::kFontSize when the
value was non-zero. As a result, the browser never saw a font size for
such text and Android did not populate an AbsoluteSizeSpan(0) on the
AccessibilityNodeInfo, even though the browser and Java code already
accept a zero font size.

Serialize the font size whenever the object has a layout object (and
therefore a computed style). AXInlineTextBox and AXValidationMessage
have no layout object, so they are unaffected.

Add a Blink dump tree test for zero font size and re-enable the
corresponding span expectation in WebContentsAccessibilityTest.

AX-Relnotes: Android: Text with CSS font-size 0 now reports a zero text size to accessibility services such as TalkBack.

[TabUI] Decouple surface scope from row projection in data provider
📋 Metadata

tree
37c20324b8fac54fa95e80b3abf002d1e7e2d94b
parent
f716246b78bcefd0f19f7bb285d4c8e2053d84bd [ diff ] 
[TabUI] Decouple surface scope from row projection in data provider

TabListDataProvider previously conflated two distinct checks under
shouldShowTab:
1. isTabInScope(tab): Does the tab pass the surface filter predicate
   (e.g., mFilter in FlatTabListDataProvider)?
2. shouldShowTab(tab): Should the tab have its own visible TabItem row
   in the list (mItems)?

In flat surfaces, every in-scope tab gets a row, so both checks match.
However, in grouped or nested surfaces, member tabs belong to the
surface (in scope for metrics and group headers) but are not rendered as
individual tab rows when collapsed.

Gating notifyTabClosureUndone and notifyTabClosureCommitted on
shouldShowTab caused valid surface events to be dropped whenever a tab
belongs to the surface but is not shown as an individual row.
Furthermore, moveTabItem accepted a raw tab ID without verifying if the
tab row is shown, requiring external visibility checks.

This CL separates the two:
* isTabInScope(tab) checks surface-level inclusion (filter predicate).
  Surface-level callbacks (notifyTabClosureUndone and
  notifyTabClosureCommitted) now check isTabInScope.
* shouldShowTab(tab) strictly checks row visibility in mItems.
* moveTabItem(tab) now takes Tab and self-guards on shouldShowTab(tab),
  removing external checks from move callbacks.

Bug: 434015906

Reland "[Omnibox] Defer AI Mode popup creation"

This is a reland of commit 7a7e498dbdb75a1c7329d8e7bf4395e3c5832fb2

The original change was reverted because
OmniboxAimPopupDeferCreationPendingBrowserTest.ClassicPopupContextMenuCreatesAimContent
crashed on mac26-arm64-rel-tests. The test opened the classic popup's
context menu while that popup was hidden, so the menu had no parent
widget, and the Mac menu code dereferences it. Real right-clicks only
reach a shown popup. The test now shows the classic popup first and
cancels the menu at the end. The production code is unchanged.

Original change's description:
> [Omnibox] Defer AI Mode popup creation
>
> Design doc:
> https://docs.google.com/document/d/1P7h7q6zqhxgPtKhVJBUAU9eb1pawDwK66iQu8CTeRSs/edit?resourcekey=0-nPCcl7S22YgfphQ-H6WV5Q&tab=t.0
>
> When a browser window is built, OmniboxPopupAimPresenter creates the AI
> Mode popup's WebUI content in its constructor: a WebContents and its
> navigation to the AI Mode popup page. The navigation's IPCs and the
> page load then compete with the new window's first frame on the browser
> UI thread and in the shared top-chrome renderer. The AI Mode popup is
> opened far less often than the regular results popup, so it does not
> need to be ready that early.
>
> This CL adds a disabled-by-default feature,
> OmniboxAimPopupDeferCreation. With it, the presenter does not create
> the content in its constructor. It overrides the new
> OmniboxPopupPresenterBase::CreateWebUIContent(), and
> OmniboxPopupPresenterBase::EnsureWebUIContentCreated() calls it on
> whichever of these comes first:
>
> - A task posted after the browser window presents its first frame.
> - OmniboxPopupPresenterBase::Show().
> - The regular popup's context menu, which acts on the AI Mode
>   WebContents.
>
> The presenter asks for that frame itself: its constructor makes a
> one-off RequestSuccessfulPresentationTimeForNextFrame() call on the
> compositor of the location bar's widget. Both LocationBarView and
> WebUILocationBar build the presenter while the window is built, before
> its first paint, so the next frame is the window's first frame. The
> presenter posts the creation task at most once.
>
> The presentation task is what keeps the work out of the window's first
> frame. A fixed delay from window creation could not do that: on Linux
> and macOS the window is activated inside its own show call, before the
> first paint, and the time to first paint varies by device. There is
> also no fallback timer. A requested callback is kept until a frame
> presents successfully. A window that is never presented never shows
> the popup either, and the on-show path covers any open. A presenter
> that is created after the first paint, because the profile became
> eligible for AI Mode later, gets the next presented frame or its first
> show.
>
> With the feature disabled, creation is unchanged.
>
>   out/Default/browser_tests \
>   --gtest_filter='OmniboxAimPopupDeferCreation*:*WithoutDeferCreation' && \
>   out/Default/unit_tests \
>   --gtest_filter='OmniboxPopupPresenterBaseTest.*'
>
> Bug: b:567847083
> Test: autoninja -C out/Default browser_tests unit_tests && \
> Change-Id: Ibb8a98bb622417b53cf22960cedef5416a6a6964
> Reviewed-on: https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8500385
> Reviewed-by: Thomas Lukaszewicz <tluk@chromium.org>
> Commit-Queue: Ming-Ying Chung <mych@chromium.org>
> Reviewed-by: Aviv Kiss <avivkiss@google.com>
> Cr-Commit-Position: refs/heads/main@{#1719187}

  out/Default/browser_tests \
  --gtest_filter='OmniboxAimPopupDeferCreation*:*WithoutDeferCreation'

[Omnibox] Add dogfood feedback button to the Full WebUI Omnibox

Adds a dogfood icon button to the Full WebUI Omnibox input, next to
the debug dino. Hovering it shows "Submit Omnibox Dogfood Feedback",
and clicking it opens Chrome's feedback form, which attaches a
screenshot, the Chrome version, platform and system info. Reports
use a new `kFeedbackSourceOmnibox` source and the `omnibox_webui`
category tag. The tag is sent as the report's bucket and matches the
"Omnibox WebUI Googler" bucket set up in gFeedback for Chrome desktop
and ChromeOS, so reports land there.

To make it clear that the feedback is about the omnibox, the form is
opened with:
- A pre-filled description: a go/omnibox-dogfood link, then the
  standard bug template headings DESCRIPTION, REPRO STEPS, EXPECTED
  RESULTS and ACTUAL RESULTS.
- Extra diagnostics listing every feature with "omnibox" or "webui"
  in its name, ignoring case, whose state was set by Finch,
  chrome://flags or the command line, with its Finch group and params.
  Features that keep their default state while tied to a trial (a
  study's Default group) are labeled "default".
  The list comes from base::FeatureList::GetFeatureOverrides(), so new
  features are picked up automatically, and params are read without
  activating trials. No user input is included.
The UI is dogfood-only, so its text (the button's tooltip and the form
template) is not translated.

The button is gated on the new `Omnibox_DogfoodFeedback` param of
`WebUIOmniboxFullPopup` (default false), so it can be enabled via
Finch for dogfood populations. It can also be enabled locally with
the "- with Dogfood Feedback" variation of
chrome://flags/#webui-omnibox-full-popup.

The WebUI calls a new `OpenDogfoodFeedback()` method on the popup
PageHandler, because the popup WebUI can't open the feedback form
itself.
The form contents and diagnostics live in the new
`omnibox_dogfood_feedback.{h,cc}`, with unit tests covering the
feature filter and the diagnostics output.

Screenshot: https://screenshot-v2.corp.google.com/2e0nrushsa6h8

TAG=agy
CONV=dea8a58e-f260-43da-8dcc-bc8ef73b95fa

parent
535782519990a3fc8f78672d2baf72b1de15e041 [ diff ] 
[Omnibox] Remove dead SearchBoxProperties.SEARCH_HINT_VISIBILITY

Remove the unused SEARCH_HINT_VISIBILITY property and its binding
branch, which were superseded by explicit hint text and alpha properties
in SearchBoxProperties.

SEARCH_HINT_VISIBILITY was originally added in 2020 so SearchBoxMediator
could hide the search box hint text whenever a Query Tile chip was
displayed inside the NTP fake omnibox. When Query Tiles were removed in
November 2021 (crrev.com/c/3290368), the SearchBoxMediator caller and
all CHIP_* properties were deleted, but SEARCH_HINT_VISIBILITY and its
SearchBoxViewBinder branch were accidentally left behind. As a result,
the property has been completely dead code with zero callers for nearly
five years.

TAG=agy
CONV=c9ac32fd-c20c-42ef-a6b1-450d9b8d8c7b

8f939bdb01c6a410bb7a320c576af4229a5bf73d

Add HaTS survey for Scheduled Restart

Integrates Happiness Tracking Surveys (HaTS) for Scheduled Restart to
measure user sentiment and friction across restart dialog choices and
entry points.

For prompt dismissals and scheduling on idle, surveys are triggered
in-session with ALLOW_ANY navigation behavior. For immediate restarts,
survey parameters are persisted to LocalState and delivered following
session restoration. Off-the-record profiles are excluded, and survey
launch is suppressed during shutdown.

TAG=agy

[ios] Automatic conversion to TypedProfileKeyedServiceFactoryIOS

This CL converts most of the ProfileKeyerServiceFactoryIOS sub-classes
to the TypedProfileKeyedServiceFactoryIOS<Factory, Service> template,
which reduces the boilerplate by automatically generating the methods
GetForProfile(), GetForProfileIfExists() and GetInstance().

The conversion was automated using the script that can be found here:
https://paste.googleplex.com/5101783618289664

The script invocation was:

  $ git grep -l 'public ProfileKeyedServiceFactoryIOS'|\
    xargs python3 convert.py && git cl format

Only the files that were correctly migrated by the script were kept.
Any files whose migration failed or caused error was either modified
to be converted automatically without error or manually converted.

This CL was uploaded by git cl split.

R=treib@chromium.org

6efa96e491e23f13a75cfc44144b8f320ecba972

author

Thu Oct 15 16:39:09 2026
committer

Thu Oct 15 16:39:09 2026
tree
ff24eb50f8f1ac0b982dc75f885fd04b93081b1e
parent
b491450b41b0e0866b45f9daffc9cf83c519a460 [ diff ] 
spanify: Fix use-site rewrites of constexpr C arrays

spanify turns `constexpr int kArr[4]` into a std::array, but rewrites
`kArr + 1` as `kArr.subspan(1u).data()`. std::array has no subspan(), so
the patch doesn't compile. `const int kArr[4]` works.

The fix has two steps:

1. Recognize the C array. For a constexpr array, the const comes from
   constexpr, so Clang wraps the ArrayTypeLoc in a QualifiedTypeLoc.
   AdaptBinaryOperation, AppendDataCall and EmitContainerPointerRewrites
   didn't unwrap it, so they saw no C array. HasBoundArrayTypeLoc()
   unwraps it, like getNodeFromArrayDecl() does for the declaration.

2. Spell the span type. Wrap the array in an explicit span, with the
   element type from ASTContext::getAsArrayType(), which keeps the
   const: `base::span<const int>(kArr).subspan(1u).data()`. The same
   applies to `&kArr[1]` passed to a spanified function, which got
   `base::span<int>` before.

The string-concat test expected the broken output: subspan() on a
std::string_view. This updates it.

In ANGLE, this fixes 15 patches that failed because of it.

b491450b41b0e0866b45f9daffc9cf83c519a460

Thu Oct 15 16:38:54 2026
tree
0f2fc85296bbc888609cfd61e5947a04cd4cde70
parent
da84957df32dd681b23ac0880d19cbc566efee06 [ diff ] 
[a11y-canvas] Fix OCR bitmap format conversion and downsampling

In AXCanvasAnnotator, canvas bitmaps passed to OCR over Mojo must
conform to skia.mojom.BitmapN32, which strictly enforces
kN32_SkColorType and tightly packed row strides (rowBytes ==
minRowBytes).

1. When a 2D canvas uses a wide-gamut float16 context,
   WebElement::ImageContents() produces non-N32 bitmaps. Furthermore,
   GPU readbacks may include row padding. Convert non-N32 bitmaps and
   repack padded rows using SkBitmap::readPixels into tightly packed N32
   format to avoid crashing Mojo serialization CHECKs.

2. Downsample canvases exceeding kMaxCanvasDimension proportionally
   using skia::ImageOperations::RESIZE_BETTER instead of dropping them
   entirely, preserving accessibility on high-DPI displays without
   causing main-thread stalls.

3. Update canvas_float16.html to use the standard context creation
   attribute 'colorType: float16'.

AX-RelNotes: n/a

716486a747c5b4548fc4a3f2f5d013a4db3bb776
[ log ]
author

Thu Oct 15 16:37:07 2026
committer

Thu Oct 15 16:37:07 2026
tree
2dd315138105ad61f2254d8051e9e04d0167e8e0
parent
3ef924420daa00243650fc0421de5e7f90b724df [ diff ] 
spanify: Rewrite span increments without auto_spanification_helper

Part 1 of removing base/containers/auto_spanification_helper.h from
spanify's output.

`++p` / `p++` on a spanified pointer used to become
base::PreIncrementSpan(p) / base::PostIncrementSpan(p). ANGLE, Dawn and
WebRTC have no such helper, so their patches did not compile.

Increments now use the span's own subspan(), the same in every project:
- value unused:           p = p.subspan(1u)
- pre-increment, used:    (p = p.subspan(1u))
- post-increment, used:   std::exchange(p, p.subspan(1u))

The whole operand is copied, including its object, so `s.ptr++` becomes
`s.ptr = s.ptr.subspan(1u)` (before: `s.base::PostIncrementSpan(ptr)`,
which doesn't compile). Since the operand is spelled twice, the pointer
is excluded if the operand has side effects, or if its text isn't one
contiguous piece of the file (e.g. part of it comes from a macro body).

Increments in a macro are rewritten in place only when spelled in an
argument of an unsafe-buffers or allow-listed (EXPECT_*, CHECK, ...)
macro. Otherwise the pointer is now excluded; before, it was still
spanified and kept a `++` that doesn't compile. Increments in an
allow-listed macro inside UNSAFE_TODO() no longer hit an assert.

GetPreIncrementSpanName() / GetPostIncrementSpanName() are removed.
Other uses of the helper are unchanged and will follow.

TAG=agy

when this notification was triggered?" section so developers have the
context needed to reproduce and resolve reported denials.

tree
bbd88e4f503a19222ba28a593d85c815359b4fd9
parent
5573e217593c32192c72875f45001da40b7f6dd2 [ diff ] 
[DBSC] Fix hang if all requests are canceled during key restore

If a request is deferred for a DBSC refresh before the session's binding
key is restored (e.g. for sessions loaded from disk), the key is
restored asynchronously first, and the request and any later ones for
the session wait in deferred_requests_. If all of them are destroyed
(e.g. canceled) in the meantime, RefreshSessionInternal() has no request
to refresh on behalf of, so it returns and leaves the abandoned entry
behind.

The earlier fix for this bug left that case as a TODO, assuming the next
refresh would clear the entry. But no refresh starts while the entry
exists, because it looks like one in flight: DeferRequestForRefresh()
queues later requests behind it, pre-warms wait on it, and proactive
refreshes are skipped. So requests for the session that need a bound
cookie hang until the session is deleted.

Drain the queue in this case with a new RefreshResult::kCanceled, as the
TODO suggested, so the next request starts a fresh refresh. A dedicated
result, rather than a failure such as kUnreachable, keeps the
DeferralResult histograms and DevTools refresh events accurate and makes
this case measurable. kCanceled is added to the mojom, CDP (experimental
Network.RefreshEventDetails) and UMA enums that mirror RefreshResult.
Pre-warms that were waiting on the abandoned refresh treat it as a
transient error and retry later.

The drain is guarded by kDeviceBoundSessionsDrainDeferredOnAllCanceled,
an enabled-by-default kill switch.

TAG=agy

[TTC] Journal page context updates and blocked URLs in TtcPageContextMonitor

This CL implements the follow-up mentioned in the comments of
crrev.com/c/8499202. TtcPageContextMonitor::NotifyPageContextFetched()
previously did not record journal entries when page context was updated
or dropped.

This CL passes SessionJournal to TtcPageContextMonitor and logs a
"TtcPageContextBlocked" entry with the page URL whenever page context
is blocked for the monitored page, and a "TtcPageContextUpdated" entry
when page context is updated.

[iOS] Migrate profile unit tests to AddManagedProfileWithBuilder

Update profile unit tests in ios/chrome/browser/cobrowse.

In Chrome on iOS, there is only one personal profile and any additional
profile must be a managed profile associated with a managed identity via
AccountProfileMapper.

This CL is in preparation for https://crrev.com/c/8579025, which removes
TestProfileIOS::Builder::SetName() and enforces that
TestProfileManagerIOS::AddProfileWithBuilder() is only used once to
create the personal profile:
- Use AddManagedProfileWithBuilder() to create managed profiles instead
  of calling AddProfileWithBuilder() multiple times with custom names.
- Remove unnecessary TestProfileIOS::Builder::SetName() calls and
  hardcoded default profile names.
- Rename profile variables to make the distinction between the personal
  profile and managed profiles explicit.

This CL was uploaded by git cl split.

R=alionadangla@chromium.org

62fba76b5a9e038d0d6e9db5367fc6934f3ded5b
parent
9719124d344750924d8738fd5992334ac9c1d39a [ diff ] 
[Autofill] Align NameInfo structure wiping and resetting with Address

Currently, `NameInfo` differs from `Address` in how it handles invalid
or updated structured subcomponents:

1. In `FinalizeNameAddressComponent()`:
 `WipeInvalidStructure()` is only called if the root component
  (`NAME_FULL` or `ALTERNATIVE_FULL_NAME`) has
  `VerificationStatus::kUserVerified`. If a profile has an invalid
  name substructure (e.g., `NAME_LAST` contains tokens not present in
  `NAME_FULL`) and `NAME_FULL` has a status other than
  `kUserVerified`, `CompleteFullTree()` fails and the stale
  subcomponents are never wiped. By contrast,
  `Address::FinalizeAfterImport()` calls `WipeInvalidStructure()`
  unconditionally whenever `CompleteFullTree()` fails.

2. In `NameInfo`, the logic that invalidates child nodes when
   overwriting `NAME_FULL` or `ALTERNATIVE_FULL_NAME` with a
   non-token-equivalent value lives in
   `SetInfoWithVerificationStatus()` rather than
   `SetRawInfoWithVerificationStatus()`.
   By contrast, `Address::SetRawInfoWithVerificationStatus()` checks
   whether `ADDRESS_HOME_STREET_ADDRESS` is already non-empty and
   invalidates its child nodes if the new value is not token-equivalent,
   with `Address::SetInfoWithVerificationStatus()` delegating to
   `SetRawInfoWithVerificationStatus()`.


This CL introduces `kAutofillWipeInvalidNameStructure` to align
`NameInfo` with `Address`:
- `FinalizeNameAddressComponent()` now calls `WipeInvalidStructure()`
  regardless of `VerificationStatus` when `CompleteFullTree()` fails.
- `NameInfo::SetRawInfoWithVerificationStatus()` now invalidates child
  nodes when overwriting a non-empty `NAME_FULL` or
  `ALTERNATIVE_FULL_NAME` with a non-token-equivalent value, and
  `NameInfo::SetInfoWithVerificationStatus()` delegates to it.

Center-align trailing link in infobar right-side container

See: https://screenshot-v2.corp.google.com/image/2g6icbf7aej8o.png

The right-side container of InfoBarView used a kEnd cross-axis
alignment. When the trailing link was shorter than the close button, the
link sat at the bottom of the container instead of lining up with the
message label and close button.

Change the cross-axis alignment of the right-side container to kCenter
so the trailing link is vertically centered relative to the other
infobar elements.

Add link_for_testing() to ConfirmInfoBar so tests can access the link.

Bug: 568732376

a6816fa3b6a8cdab7112adc3f109d62404839da5
parent
d08475f3e580d230e75cede0aaf968a9676d2d90 [ diff ] 
Reland "WebMCP: Re-check for a duplicate tool name after serializing inputSchema"

This is a reland of commit bbf032940645a5dcea55e6dc1bdb34b8c989650f,
which was reverted in 8a0f949dda972 for breaking the build.

The original CL was written against a `ModelContext` whose `tool_map_`
was keyed by tool name, and checked for a duplicate with
`tool_map_.find(tool->name())`. https://crrev.com/c/8510757 landed in
between and changed `tool_map_` to be keyed by tool ID, so that no
longer compiled. The reland uses `FindToolDataByName()`, just like the
up-front duplicate check does.

Original change's description:
> WebMCP: Re-check for a duplicate tool name after serializing inputSchema
>
> `registerTool()` rejects a duplicate tool name up front, but then
> serializes `inputSchema`, which can run script (via `toJSON()`) that
> registers another tool with the same name. `registerTool()` then still
> sends the duplicate to the browser, which kills the renderer.
>
> Check for a duplicate name again after serialization, next to the
> existing document-activity re-check, and reject with the same
> `InvalidStateError` as the up-front check.
>
> See https://github.com/webmachinelearning/webmcp/pull/347 for the spec
> fix.
>
> R=khushalsagar
>
> Bug: 573722078
> Change-Id: I044e3ad96fb00c624829eb182047aa30575f81a6

R=khushalsagar

used the browser's UI language), even when previewing a voice in another
language.

cc9ddd9ce868ae5fda73d1fdd7bb419d07daaafd
parent
6563f89211c484d3cfb7866d875045d806e505a4 [ diff ] 
[iOS] Reuse NotifyingView across window moves

When Chrome enters the Tab Grid while in the background,
-[TabGridTransitionHandler snapshotOfViewPortionAboveRect:middleRect:]
snapshots _browserLayoutViewController.view with afterScreenUpdates:YES.
UIKit's _performAfterContextCommitUnderCoverAllowDefer saves the layout
constraints on ChromeOverlayWindow referencing the internal
NotifyingView mirror before temporarily moving the browser layout view
to a cover window, removing it from the cover window (window == nil),
and restoring it to ChromeOverlayWindow before reactivating the saved
constraints via _UIViewReactivatePotentiallyDisruptedConstraintsForView.

Previously, addMirrorViewInWindow() and windowCoordinatesChangeHandler()
called removeMirrorViewInWindow() and allocated a new NotifyingView
whenever the observed view moved between windows, leaving the original
NotifyingView detached (superview == nil) when UIKit reactivated the
saved constraints on ChromeOverlayWindow.

Reuse the existing mirrorViewInWindow instance in
addMirrorViewInWindow() and only remove it from its superview when
self.window becomes nil, preserving the instance until
cr_onWindowCoordinatesChanged is cleared.

[CE]Prevent crash when frame is detached during capability element registration

Creating the embedded permission request descriptor can trigger layout
updates or script execution that detaches the frame or execution
context.

This change invokes CreateEmbeddedPermissionRequestDescriptor earlier
and verifies that the frame, rendered state, receiver binding, and
execution context remain valid before proceeding with registration or
permission requests.

[iOS][TTC] Log upstream audio telemetry in TtcConversation

Microphone capture produces high-frequency PCM chunks that would
overwhelm the session journal if logged individually. Aggregate upstream
audio into per-burst kAudio journal events: log AudioInputStarted on the
first chunk of a burst and flush an AudioInputSent summary (chunk count,
byte volume, and peak input energy) when the burst ends. Also log
deduplicated AudioInputSuppressed and AudioInputDropped events when
capture is gated by active playback or a disconnected backend.

tree
4c8db8f6aee210cd9342ebab6d14fca5ec1c69fa
parent
6af88832f6ce51348fe6731f2c24eceb28976356 [ diff ] 
[WebUI Toolbar] Check active tab WebContents in LocationIconTestAccessor::IsBubbleShowing()

LocationIconTestAccessor::IsBubbleShowing() called GetWebContents(),
which actually returned the WebUI toolbar's internal WebContents rather
than the browser's active tab WebContents.

Since WebUILocationBar::ShowPageInfoBubble() associates the Page Info
bubble with the active tab's WebContents, IsBubbleShowing() (and
ShowBubble()) always returned false when WebUILocationBar was enabled,
causing GenericMimeHandlerChipBrowserTest.ChipClickOpensInternalPageInfo
to fail under --enable-features=InitialWebUI,WebUIToolbar.

This CL updates LocationIconTestAccessor::IsBubbleShowing() to query
PageInfoBubbleViewBase::IsShowing() with the active tab's WebContents,
and renames the private helper to GetToolbarWebContents() for clarity.

4f0c3b4457b125cf6a95d81afac9eafc3dc28c1d [ diff ] 
Add experimentalAiData.getTextSurroundingSelection API

This introduces the `getTextSurroundingSelection` method to the
experimentalAiData extension API, allowing privileged extensions to
retrieve text surrounding the current selection in a target tab up to a
specified maximum length.

- Moves `GetTabById` to `ExperimentalAiDataApiFunction` for reuse.
- Implements `ExperimentalAiDataGetTextSurroundingSelectionFunction`,
  which queries the focused frame (or primary main frame if none is
  focused) for surrounding text via `RequestTextSurroundingSelection`.
- Adds WebIDL definitions, histogram entries, unit tests, and browser
  tests for the new API function.

[a11y] Add forced-colors hover outline and icon border to cr-url-list-item

In forced-colors (High Contrast) mode, Blink strips non-system
background-color values and forces them to transparent, while
AddNativeHighContrastColors() maps kColorSysStateHoverOnSubtle to
SK_ColorTRANSPARENT. As a result, <cr-url-list-item> cards (used in the
Bookmarks Side Panel and Reading List) had no visible hover state and no
visible boundary around #iconContainer.

This CL updates the @media (forced-colors: active) block in
cr_url_list_item.css to:
- Add a 1px solid Highlight outline on :host(.hovered) and
  :host([force-hover]).
- Add a 1px solid ButtonBorder border on #iconContainer.

Demo: https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NDkxNzQzMDE0Mzk0MjY1Nnw4ZTgwNGJmOC01Zg

411b89a5b9c026e3c6a8ebcba43f0cfd5aa25ac8
parent
8bba66ea56b9033c47131c4ae479fc82a66263b4 [ diff ] 
jni_zero: Kotlin outputs

Since kotlinc doesn't allow a kotlin class to be the parent of an
already compiled java class (the @NativeMethods annotated inner class
in Foo.kt should be the parent of FooJni), we need FooJni.java to also
be able to be a kotlin file. Kotlinc will then allow lazy resolution of
the parent, and won't freak out when it can't find it in our generated
files, and will happily look to our now-compiling .kt file.

[ios] Automatic conversion to TypedProfileKeyedServiceFactoryIOS

This CL converts most of the ProfileKeyerServiceFactoryIOS sub-classes
to the TypedProfileKeyedServiceFactoryIOS<Factory, Service> template,
which reduces the boilerplate by automatically generating the methods
GetForProfile(), GetForProfileIfExists() and GetInstance().

The conversion was automated using the script that can be found here:
https://paste.googleplex.com/5101783618289664

The script invocation was:

  $ git grep -l 'public ProfileKeyedServiceFactoryIOS'|\
    xargs python3 convert.py && git cl format

Only the files that were correctly migrated by the script were kept.
Any files whose migration failed or caused error was either modified
to be converted automatically without error or manually converted.

This CL was uploaded by git cl split.

R=battre@chromium.org

12458647cf447b3473f31d6da51c7129013fd533 [ diff ] 
Check for context provider before creating ExternalCanvasResource

This moves condition that triggers returning nullptr up:
* resource->is_cross_thread() is false by definition, as it checks if
  we're not on the creation thread.
* resource->context_provider_wrapper_ is context_provider_wrapper.

Now we skip ctor and dtor of ExternalCanvasResource if there is no
context provider:
* ctor does nothing except setting fields
* dtor calls release callback with destruction_sync_token_, which would
  be empty [1]

So we run callback before returning nullptr.

[1] https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:third_party/blink/renderer/platform/graphics/canvas_resource.h;drc=6b42a73b1537663535d7ab65396c915b6720378d;l=291

Autofill: Introduce Autocomplete Key Metrics for FillingAcceptance and FillingCorrectness

This CL introduces Key Metrics for Autocomplete modeled after Autofill.KeyMetrics.*:
- Autocomplete.KeyMetrics.FillingAcceptance{MatchingType}
- Autocomplete.KeyMetrics.FillingCorrectness{MatchingType}
These metrics are recorded per field upon form submission:
- FillingAcceptance records whether the user accepted an autocomplete suggestion
  for a field where autocomplete suggestions were shown.
- FillingCorrectness records, for fields where an autocomplete suggestion was
  accepted, whether the filled value was submitted without manual edits.
Both metrics emit overall aggregate counts and, when
kAutofillLabelSensitiveAutocomplete is enabled, are broken down by matching type:
- .BothNameAndLabel
- .Label
- .Name
TAG=agy
CONV=8ac7b87f-8564-41eb-a1a5-863351ef5ae4

  its view in TabCollectionNode::MoveChild().

[Win] Set Crashpad sampling state once after field trials are ready

Previously, ChromeMetricsServicesManagerClient::UpdateRunningServices()
called install_static::SetCollectStatsInSample() on every invocation
(e.g. on every browser window creation/closure via
IncognitoObserverDesktop and on profile creation).

Since IsClientInSampleForCrashes() only depends on the
kMetricsReportingFeature field trial state, which does not change after
field trials are initialized, move the
install_static::SetCollectStatsInSample() call to a new
ChromeMetricsServicesManagerClient::OnFieldTrialsInitialized() method
called once from ChromeFeatureListCreator::SetUpFieldTrials().

This is a precursor to https://crrev.com/c/8506542.

parent
afe4cec60c233f8534e10b7e05ac194a002581f6 [ diff ] 
omnibox: Skip permission prompt dismissal handling when popup is hidden

On macOS, hiding the browser window during teardown orders out child
popup widgets before updating the parent window's visibility state.
When an active permission prompt is attached to the AIM popup's
WebContents, hiding the child widget marks its WebContents as hidden
and triggers PermissionRequestManager::DeletePrompt() ->
OnPromptRemoved(). Previously, HandlePermissionPromptDismissal()
unconditionally refocused the location bar even when the popup was
already hidden, triggering LocationBarView::ValidatePopupState(kAim)
while the AIM widget was hidden and the parent browser widget still
reported visible.

Skip entering prompt dismissal mode and refocusing the location bar in
HandlePermissionPromptDismissal() when IsShown() is false.

[ios] Automatic conversion to TypedProfileKeyedServiceFactoryIOS

This CL converts most of the ProfileKeyerServiceFactoryIOS sub-classes
to the TypedProfileKeyedServiceFactoryIOS<Factory, Service> template,
which reduces the boilerplate by automatically generating the methods
GetForProfile(), GetForProfileIfExists() and GetInstance().

The conversion was automated using the script that can be found here:
https://paste.googleplex.com/5101783618289664

The script invocation was:

  $ git grep -l 'public ProfileKeyedServiceFactoryIOS'|\
    xargs python3 convert.py && git cl format

Only the files that were correctly migrated by the script were kept.
Any files whose migration failed or caused error was either modified
to be converted automatically without error or manually converted.

This CL was uploaded by git cl split.

R=qpubert@google.com

📝 Commit Message (First Line)
Linux: Give non-default profiles a per-profile WM_CLASS/app_id
📋 Metadata
commit
2237b5ee299801e0fc08a33eb18c2fa88b806a1f
[ log ]
author
Tom Anderson <thomasanderson@chromium.org>
Thu Oct 15 15:34:09 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Thu Oct 15 15:34:09 2026
tree
e378ec26bb0807aa5ada308f991e6bd6a70838d7
parent
fa8fea90a7f3d803cc2dde2fef7d921e0cef27c6 [ diff ] 
Linux: Give non-default profiles a per-profile WM_CLASS/app_id

All browser windows report the same X11 WM_CLASS and Wayland app_id
regardless of profile, so desktop shells can't match windows of
different profiles to per-profile launchers and icons.

Behind the new LinuxPerProfileAppId feature, browser windows of a
non-default profile in the default user data directory now use a
per-profile desktop entry ID, e.g. "google-chrome-Profile_1", as the
X11 WM_CLASS res_name and the Wayland app_id. The res_class is left
unchanged, and the default profile keeps the browser's main identity,
like the AppUserModelID on Windows.

The ID is the browser's desktop entry name followed by the profile
directory name. It is used as a .desktop file name, a Latin-1 WM_CLASS
and a Wayland app_id, none of which limit its length, but characters
other than ASCII letters, digits and '-' in the profile directory name
are replaced with '_', following the desktop entry spec's
recommendation to use D-Bus names as desktop entry IDs.

The per-profile ID is only used if a desktop entry with that name is
installed. A Wayland app_id without a matching .desktop file leaves
the window without an icon or launcher association on GNOME and KDE
(see crbug.com/41495747), so this can't regress users who don't have
per-profile launchers. Installed entries are detected at startup,
before any browser window is created.

7c1a8ba584dc0786e66aebd4ae8ae94dbde7c7ab
parent
a122221b1fc7d32159802d829c63e7a67e5fd36d [ diff ] 
[ash] Drop dead chrome/browser/apps/app_service includes from ash code

Thirty-five includes of the app_service headers in ash directories no
longer name anything from them, mostly app_service_proxy.h and
app_service_proxy_factory.h.

Found by removing every candidate and letting the compiler decide: a
textual filter alone is misleading here, because a file that writes
AppServiceProxyFactory::GetForProfile(profile)->Foo() needs the proxy
definition without ever naming it. Of 115 candidates the compiler
rejected 80, and those are kept.

As a sample of what one of these costs, preprocessing
app_list/search/keyboard_shortcut_result.cc goes from 222114 lines and
1253 distinct headers to 216677 lines and 1203 headers once its unused
app_service_proxy.h include is gone.

This sheds no DEPS allowance: every directory touched still has other
users of chrome/browser/apps. It is include cleanup rather than
decoupling.

#ai-assisted

[iOS][Actor] Post ActorService lifecycle notifications

Posts `ActorTaskLifecycleObserver` notifications through
`PostedObserverList` so observers cannot re-enter `ActorService` on its
call stack (e.g. stopping a task from
`actorServiceDidStartTaskWithID:`). Start notifications are no longer
delivered synchronously in `CreateTask()`.

Also adds a sequence checker to `ActorService`, since
`PostedObserverList` requires single-sequence use.

[Memory Pressure] Add macOS system memory breakdown histograms

Before this CL, the only macOS-specific system memory histogram was
Memory.Experimental.MacFileBackedMemoryMB2, expired since March 2025.
The cross-platform AvailableMemory* histograms fold free and file-backed
memory into one number, which on macOS hides two quantities that precede
swapping: how much of RAM the compressor occupies and how much of it is
wired and therefore out of play.

This CL records a per-category breakdown every 2 minutes from a single
GetSystemMemoryInfo() snapshot. It un-expires MacFileBackedMemoryMB2 and
adds Memory.Experimental.Mac{Free,Speculative,Purgeable,Wired,
Unwired,Compressor,CompressorUncompressed,CompressorSwapped}MemoryMB,
the matching Mac{FileBacked,Free,Wired}MemoryPercent, and
MacCompressorPercentOf{Total,Unwired}. Disk swap is read directly from
the vm.swapusage sysctl as MacSwap{Used,Total}MB, since SystemMemoryInfo
does not report swap on Apple platforms.

The recording moves into a RecordMacSystemMemoryMetrics() helper in
metrics_provider_common.cc under BUILDFLAG(IS_MAC). The unit test drives
it through the existing 2 minute RepeatingTimer with MOCK_TIME rather
than exposing the private method.

0ac9ceaaa1e4f8ba8083a1bb59946de243d0ae37 [ diff ] 
Fix flaky ReadAnythingUntrustedPageHandlerPdfTranslationStateTest

ReadAnythingUntrustedPageHandlerTest::SetUpOnMainThread() opens a new
foreground tab to about:blank and previously only waited for
DidStopLoading via BROWSER_TEST_WAIT_FOR_LOAD_STOP. At the end of
NavigateToURLWithDispositionBlockUntilNavigationsComplete(),
SimulateEndOfPaintHoldingOnPrimaryMainFrame() triggers a renderer
lifecycle update (DidMeaningfulLayout -> TranslateAgent::PageCaptured),
which sends an asynchronous ContentTranslateDriver::RegisterPage Mojo
IPC to the browser process.

In tests such as OnHandlerConstructed_TabTranslated_SendsTranslated that
immediately mutate the tab's LanguageState via TranslateTab() and spin a
RunLoop during FlushAndVerifyPage(), the delayed RegisterPage IPC from
the initial about:blank navigation can arrive mid-test and call
LanguageState::LanguageDetermined("", false), resetting
is_page_translated_ to false and firing an unexpected
OnTranslationStateChanged(false) notification.

Fix this by waiting for TranslateWaiter::WaitEvent::kLanguageDetermined
on the initial about:blank tab in SetUpOnMainThread() before starting
the test body.

Test has been moderately flaky since it was introduced -
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/test/chromium%3Aci/%3A%2F%2Fchrome%2Ftest%5C%3Abrowser_tests%21gtest%3A%3AReadAnythingUntrustedPageHandlerPdfTranslationStateTest%23OnHandlerConstructed_TabTranslated_SendsTranslated?q=VHASH%3Aefe34715356dfeed

918a83487c441cd98bcf1090ea8d21681d5808f0 [ diff ] 
[ios] Automatic conversion to TypedProfileKeyedServiceFactoryIOS

This CL converts most of the ProfileKeyerServiceFactoryIOS sub-classes
to the TypedProfileKeyedServiceFactoryIOS<Factory, Service> template,
which reduces the boilerplate by automatically generating the methods
GetForProfile(), GetForProfileIfExists() and GetInstance().

The conversion was automated using the script that can be found here:
https://paste.googleplex.com/5101783618289664

The script invocation was:

  $ git grep -l 'public ProfileKeyedServiceFactoryIOS'|\
    xargs python3 convert.py && git cl format

Only the files that were correctly migrated by the script were kept.
Any files whose migration failed or caused error was either modified
to be converted automatically without error or manually converted.

This CL was uploaded by git cl split.

R=gayane@google.com

[CloseAllTabs] Retain pinned tabs on GTS Close All Tabs with opt-in checkbox

When closing all tabs from the Grid Tab Switcher (GTS) 3-dot overflow
menu, pinned tabs were unconditionally closed alongside unpinned tabs.

Behind the AndroidCloseAllTabsRetainsPinnedTabs feature flag:
- Add TabModelUtils.hasUnpinnedTabs(TabModel) and
  TabModelUtils.getUnpinnedTabs(TabModel) to enumerate and check for
  unpinned tabs when closing all tabs.
- Update CloseAllTabsHelper.closeAllTabsHidingTabGroups and
  CloseAllTabsHelper.buildCloseAllTabsCallback to retain pinned regular
  tabs by default via TabClosureParams.closeTabs(unpinnedTabs)
  .hideTabGroups(true), skipping regular tab closure when no unpinned
  tabs remain after unarchiving archived tabs.
- Update CloseAllTabsDialog.show to accept an explicit isIncognitoOnly
  parameter, display an "Also close pinned tabs" checkbox (unchecked by
  default) when closing regular tabs with pinned tabs present, disable
  the positive button via a PropertyObserver on CHECKBOX_CHECKED when
  only pinned tabs are open, and record the
  Tab.CloseAllTabsDialog.AlsoClosePinnedTabs UMA histogram.

SS: https://screenshot-v2.corp.google.com/7jtsa37pe1ms0

Revert "Reland "[BottomSheet] Fix wrap-content measurement and viewport bottom inset""

This reverts commit 3bd23a74d5c0ab22b0afe76f667eb1321d2100a9.

Reason for revert: too many issues. Broke read aloud and possibly the archived tabs IPH too.

Original change's description:
> Reland "[BottomSheet] Fix wrap-content measurement and viewport bottom inset"
>
> This reverts commit 2500f440508f65f8bef5a9f1e6179f040e2e77e6.
>
> Reason for revert: double counted E2E insets.
>
> Original change's description:
> > Revert "[BottomSheet] Fix wrap-content measurement and viewport bottom inset"
> >
> > This reverts commit 6430aa3a5ec3f69eba7dafe7f22d0569b92e4b93.
> >
> > Reason for revert: Suspected cause of breakage on android-12-x64-rel and android-10-x86-rel
> >
> > Failure Link: https://ci.chromium.org/p/chromium/builders/ci/android-10-x86-rel/b8668492391627102897
> >
> > Original change's description:
> > > [BottomSheet] Fix wrap-content measurement and viewport bottom inset
> > >
> > > 1. In `getViewportBottomInset()`, guard against non-empty
> > >         `mVisibleViewportRect` rather than `isSheetOpen()`. When
> > >         opening a `WRAP_CONTENT` sheet in landscape (where
> > >         `e2eBottomInset == 0` and navigation bar bottom inset is
> > >         non-zero), `isSheetOpen()` was false during initial
> > >         `showContent()` measurement, causing
> > >         `getViewportBottomInset()` to return 0. Using
> > >         `!mVisibleViewportRect.isEmpty()` ensures the bottom inset is
> > >         accounted for immediately during initial wrap-content
> > >         measurement. In `calculateSheetTranslationY()`, also zero
> > >         `bottomInsetAdjustment` when `currentOffsetPx <= 0`.
> > >
> > > 2. In `updateViewportAndBottomInset()`, call
> > >         `invalidateContentDesiredHeight()` when
> > >         `insetOrHeightChanged` is true so container height or
> > >         viewport inset changes seen by a layout pass trigger
> > >         remeasurement of `WRAP_CONTENT` sheet content. In
> > >         `onInsetChanged()`, do the same when the viewport bottom
> > >         inset differs from the one seen by the last layout pass, and
> > >         re-wrap an open `WRAP_CONTENT` sheet so that inset animations
> > >         resettle it. `mPreviousViewportBottomInset` is still only
> > >         updated by the layout pass, so the next layout pass still
> > >         detects the change.
> > >
> > > 3. Add `getSystemBarOrEdgeToEdgeBottomInset()`, which returns
> > >         the larger of the edge-to-edge bottom inset and the bottom
> > >         system bars inset (or 0 for large form factor sheets and
> > >         sheets with a bottom margin), without adding the keyboard
> > >         height. Use it in `getContentBottomPadding()` and pass it to
> > >         `mMediator.calculateSheetTranslationY()` in
> > >         `setSheetOffsetFromBottom()`. In landscape mode where
> > >         `e2eBottomInset` is 0 but navigation bar bottom inset is
> > >         non-zero, this accounts for the bottom system bar inset. As
> > >         with the edge-to-edge inset before, the translation also
> > >         applies to `RESIZE_CONTENT` sheets.
> > >
> > > 4. In `ensureContentDesiredHeightIsComputed()`, subtract both
> > >         `getHandlebarHeight()` and `getContentBottomPadding()` from
> > >         `getMaxSheetHeight()` when measuring `WRAP_CONTENT` sheet
> > >         content with `MeasureSpec.AT_MOST`.
> > >
> > > Before:
> > > https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NTExMzE2MDE5MjIyOTM3NnwwNTdhZDdjNi0wZg
> > > https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NjE1MDI3OTc2MTc1NjE2MHxkYzQxNTFiMC0wNA
> > > After:
> > > https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NTM1MTY1MDA5NjA1NDI3Mnw0YWMxYTZiYS1hMg
> > >
> > > Bug: 530492213, 531670148

tree
12a1857a2441c326f924846cf5e5785bcb2afa51

b41e74262ee1742300cb416834187ac6b54bb054 [ diff ] 
[DevTools] Enforce URL policy on frame and Android navigations

- Enforce DeveloperToolsAvailabilityBlocklist and Allowlist policies on
  frame targets across same-document navigations, on OOPIFs by their own
  URL, and on Android across all committed navigations.
- Previously, committed same-document navigations (such as
  history.pushState) only re-validated tab targets on desktop, subframe
  policy checks evaluated the owning WebContents rather than the frame
  URL, and Android did not enforce frame-level policy checks.
- Re-validate frame-target sessions on committed same-document
  navigations, evaluate each frame's committed URL under target-level
  policy evaluation before falling back to WebContents, and override
  AllowInspectingRenderFrameHost on Android.

Fixed: 502803648

tree
796274b3cdb65ee006f1de2d177859e83073ac43
parent
06fa863c22a03f63e304fb2fe5361642371e7252 [ diff ] 
[chrome://policy] Implement policy conversion for the mojo policy type

Adds strongly typed Mojo equivalents of the base::Value based policy
conversions, as the next step in migrating chrome://policy from untyped
WebUI messages to Mojo. Builds on the parent CL, which moved shared
logic into helpers used by both the base::Value and Mojo paths.
policy.mojom:
- Adds PolicyLevel and PolicyScope enums, mirroring policy::PolicyLevel
  and policy::PolicyScope, and uses them in Policy and Conflict instead
  of strings. The legacy "allUsers" scope was only produced on Lacros
  and is not carried over (cleanup tracked in crbug.com/571819020).
PolicyConversionsClient:
- Adds GetChromePoliciesMojo(), GetPrecedencePoliciesMojo(),
  GetPrecedenceOrderMojo(), GetPolicyMojo() and GetPolicyValuesMojo().
- The Mojo output is independent of the base::Value formatting options:
  level and scope are always enums (EnableConvertTypes() is ignored),
  and values are never turned into JSON strings (EnableConvertValues()
  is ignored). Sensitive and hidden machine values are still masked
  via the new CopyAndMask() helper, which CopyAndMaybeConvert() now
  builds on.
- Conflicts are described by mojom::Conflict (level, scope, source,
  value), which is all the UI uses, rather than a nested full policy.
PolicyConversions:
- Adds ToMojoPolicyGroups(), implemented by ChromePolicyConversions
  only for now. Other delegates hit NOTREACHED() until they are
  migrated.
WebUI:
- The frontend uses the mojom enums for level and scope. Until
  policies are sent over Mojo, convertLegacyLevelAndScope() converts
  the legacy 'policies-updated' strings to the enums.
The chrome://policy handler still sends policies over the legacy WebUI
path; switching it to ToMojoPolicyGroups() will happen in a follow-up.

NO_IFTTT=Only adding a comment

a20e6136cc166e91400b6ff6b6a054a3013d2c7d [ diff ] 
[Omnibox] Document ORP single-instance and caching rules in GEMINI.md

Production code must use the single shared OmniboxResourceProvider;
test files may create one instance and must reuse it, switching color
schemes via setBrandedColorScheme().

Describe when ORP instance methods may resolve through ResourceCache:
only for values that the updateStateAndCache() reset logic (layout size
bucket, locale, night mode, densityDpi, fontScale) re-evaluates. Branded
color scheme changes do not reset the cache, so scheme-dependent
resources must be selected by resource ID before the cache lookup.

parent
54e5f786b42a61f13510f0d4753e8f8dfe067713 [ diff ] 
[Reading mode PDFs] Restore reading order for out-of-order PDF blocks

When `PdfAccessibilityHeuristicEnhancements` is enabled:
- Reject backward-X jumps between left-to-right runs in
  `LineHelper::ContinuesLineInReadingOrder()` so side-by-side columns
  are not merged into a single line.
- Break paragraphs in `BreakParagraphByLineSpacing()` on upward-Y jumps
  or same-line backward-X jumps between left-to-right runs.
- Reorder page block nodes into top-to-bottom vertical bands via
  `SortBlockNodesByVerticalBands()` while preserving column-major stream
  order across side-by-side columns.

624e4aaabf4eebaf02579c30495b195da0ef2c93

e98cc24410dc2be12385df5ebcf9b737ce0d65c6 [ diff ] 
Remove allowoptimization from Cronet ProGuard

The semantics of `allowoptimization` are not well-defined, and the
ambiguity can lead to breakages - for example, users have reported that
DexGuard can remove parameters when this option is used, causing runtime
crashes as the JNI call doesn't match the Java signature anymore.

dcda90624dbcf8ef6a9970f4d47889fa3d005df4 [ diff ] 
[ios] Automatic conversion to TypedProfileKeyedServiceFactoryIOS

This CL converts most of the ProfileKeyerServiceFactoryIOS sub-classes
to the TypedProfileKeyedServiceFactoryIOS<Factory, Service> template,
which reduces the boilerplate by automatically generating the methods
GetForProfile(), GetForProfileIfExists() and GetInstance().

The conversion was automated using the script that can be found here:
https://paste.googleplex.com/5101783618289664

The script invocation was:

  $ git grep -l 'public ProfileKeyedServiceFactoryIOS'|\
    xargs python3 convert.py && git cl format

Only the files that were correctly migrated by the script were kept.
Any files whose migration failed or caused error was either modified
to be converted automatically without error or manually converted.

This CL was uploaded by git cl split.

R=christianxu@chromium.org

📋 Metadata

04e37fbf70e750cc9a80af0fb0ec92d8ef6bd780
[ log ]
author
David Bokan <bokan@chromium.org>
Thu Oct 15 14:53:02 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Thu Oct 15 14:53:02 2026
tree
1d364c552fac0f88616379a0091f1caafa38538a
parent
0b080bfb53e2be7f4ac5228c7222da68d7f77fe6 [ diff ] 
actor: Remove redundant AddActorSurface calls

Since kGlicEarlyAddTaskTabs made it so that the tab (now ActorSurface)
is added early in ActorTask::Act, we no longer need to call
AddActorSurface after this point.

Tools doing so from UpdateTaskBeforeInvoke no longer need to because
that's called from ExecutionEngine, after ActorTask::Act. Similarly for
the two calls in ExecutionEngine itself.

The only case to consider closely here is
ExecutionEngine::EnqueueFollowupAction which adds an Action that doesn't
go through ActorTask::Act. The only users of this method though are
AttemptLoginToolRequest and AttemptFormFillingToolRequest, both of which
are themselves TabToolRequests and enqueue an action on the same tab (so
that tab must already have been added). Indeed, any future such tool
must also make itself a TabToolRequest.

tree
1dacd4a1a551911b5496211a84040c31993856e9
parent
c8af3f9393ccdf07b4efb36191e8fd78d3f8626e [ diff ] 
[OTP] Remove OneTimeTokenSource.

`OneTimeTokenSource` is redundant with `OneTimeTokenType`:
`OneTimeTokenService::Subscribe()` only delivers on-device SMS OTPs, and
`GetRecentOneTimeTokens()` derives the source of each token from its
type.

This CL removes `OneTimeTokenSource` and drops it from
`OneTimeTokenService::Callback`. `OtpManagerImpl` and
`OtpManagerLegacyImpl` now tell SMS and Gmail OTPs apart via
`OneTimeToken::type()`. As errors carry no token (and hence no type),
`OnOneTimeTokenReceived()` now handles them first.

Slight behavior changes (none of them expected to be user-visible):
- Tokens are classified by their type instead of by the backend that
  delivered them. In practice, these always coincide: the SMS OTP
  backend creates `kSmsOtp` tokens and `GmailOtpBackend` creates
  `kGmail` tokens.
- All errors are handled like errors of the SMS OTP flow, which never
  consume the pending suggestions callback of `OtpManagerImpl` while a
  PhishGuard check for a Gmail OTP is in flight. Previously, errors
  attributed to Gmail would have consumed it, but no such errors are
  reported: `OnGmailOtpRetrieved()` drops retrieval errors.
- `OtpManagerLegacyImpl` ignores Gmail OTPs after rather than before
  checking for a pending callback and for errors. Neither check has
  side effects for Gmail OTPs, so this is not observable.

[webauthn] Fix opportunistic key retrieval before EnclaveManager load

Ensure EnclaveManager::StoreKeysFromOutOfContextRetrieval loads
persistent enclave state before checking whether the current user is
already registered.

This fixes a bug where storing known keys from opportunistic retrieval
before an EnclaveManager would wedge the state machine.

Also defensively ensure the state machine is stopped upon storing keys,
even when no new secrets were obtained and no domain join are needed.

511a707049e3b0b71d52eff5a9e104e9650fc6b6 [ diff ] 
Android: fetch the Linux JDK on non-Linux hosts

Java actions sent to remote execution run on Linux workers, but a Mac
checkout only has the macOS JDK in //third_party/jdk/current. Like
//third_party/node, also fetch the Linux JDK into
//third_party/jdk/linux-amd64 on non-Linux hosts, so it can be uploaded
for those actions. Both JDKs share jdk_version so they roll together.
Nothing uses the new directory yet; a follow-up selects it for remote
Java actions.

Migrate more Password Manager  callers to DeviceAuthAvailabilityChecker

This CL migrates mostly UI callers (Touch-to-Fill, Keyboard Accessory,
All Passwords bottom sheet, Credential Manager) and remote actor
permission controller to use DeviceAuthAvailabilityChecker instead of
the legacy DeviceAuthenticator query method.

Each call site checks kDeviceReauthRefactor to fall back to the legacy
path when the feature is disabled, and includes unit tests for both the
enabled and disabled paths.

TAG=agy

b08574c2181df52809c8ac3a661eda737a31fc0d
parent
f7649192d2fb57b7e2c47eceb8c618e99fcf1a98 [ diff ] 
[Signin] Record HistorySyncOptIn Started and Completed metrics

Expose HistorySyncOptinHelper::RecordMetricsForHistorySyncStarted() and
HistorySyncOptinHelper::RecordMetricsForHistorySyncUserChoice(), and
call them from signin_ui_util::SignInAndEnableHistorySync() when
enabling history sync (immediately when already signed in,
web-signed-in, or sign-in pending, or recording Started when opening
the sign-in tab and Completed via SigninPromoTabHelper after completing
sign-in from the signed-out state).

Previously, inline history sync promos on chrome://history,
chrome://history/syncedTabs, and collaboration tab groups enabled
history sync without recording Signin.HistorySyncOptIn.Started,
Signin_HistorySync_Started, Signin.HistorySyncOptIn.Completed, or
Signin_HistorySync_Completed.

Record the completion metric in SignInAndEnableHistorySync() rather
than inside signin_util::EnableHistorySync() because
EnableHistorySync() is also called from
HistorySyncOptinHandler::Accept() (where HistorySyncOptinHelper already
records the completion metrics and does not pass the access point to
HistorySyncOptinHandler) and from
HistorySyncOptinService::OnPrimaryAccountChanged() (which re-applies
EnableHistorySync() for managed accounts after
EnsureManagedProfileForAccount() completes, after
SignInAndEnableHistorySync() or SigninPromoTabHelper has already run).
Recording inside EnableHistorySync() would therefore double-count
completions in those flows.

Fixed: 570729144
Test: browser_tests --gtest_filter=*HistorySyncOptinTest*

tree
a9cf86db64c3cbf693f9b25faf9db20cee1d1143
parent
a26e61145ce19c8644887266dc5bf40b320af236 [ diff ] 
[headless] Do not mutate process CommandLine in ProcessHeadlessCommands

headless::ProcessHeadlessCommands() previously appended
--disable-lazy-loading switch to base::CommandLine::ForCurrentProcess()
during browser startup after worker threads had already started, causing
a data race with concurrent HasSwitch() reads on background threads.

This CL removes the runtime CommandLine mutation and instead checks
headless::ShouldProcessHeadlessCommands() directly in
ChromeContentBrowserClient::OverrideWebPreferences() to disable lazy
loading when executing headless commands.

Bug: 572111820

[iOS] Migrate profile unit tests to AddManagedProfileWithBuilder

Update profile unit tests in ios/chrome/browser/metrics/model.

In Chrome on iOS, there is only one personal profile and any additional
profile must be a managed profile associated with a managed identity via
AccountProfileMapper.

This CL is in preparation for https://crrev.com/c/8579025, which removes
TestProfileIOS::Builder::SetName() and enforces that
TestProfileManagerIOS::AddProfileWithBuilder() is only used once to
create the personal profile:
- Use AddManagedProfileWithBuilder() to create managed profiles instead
  of calling AddProfileWithBuilder() multiple times with custom names.
- Remove unnecessary TestProfileIOS::Builder::SetName() calls and
  hardcoded default profile names.
- Rename profile variables to make the distinction between the personal
  profile and managed profiles explicit.

This CL was uploaded by git cl split.

R=olivierrobin@chromium.org

[iOS] Build model picker context menu from ComposeboxModelOptionSet

ComposeboxMenuContextMenuBuilder previously hardcoded a static list of
model item types in -createModelPickerMenu. As a result, newly supported
model options (such as ComposeboxModelOption::kProLatest) did not appear
in the model picker context menu.

Iterate over ComposeboxModelOptionSet::All() (excluding kNone) to build
the context menu dynamically from all available model options.

7c14c726bf3d2130faac026ccd9fff4312a957e9
parent
4104e7e42e2eb9352d9f0d9a218445387ba6e744 [ diff ] 
[Extensions] Allow component frames in BFCache

Extension frames were previously blocked from entering
BackForwardCache because extension pages share a single static
`WindowAgent` across a renderer process, so freezing a cached tab's
microtask queue would stall active foreground tabs or side panels in
the same process. Component extensions opting in via
`"back_forward_cache": { "supported": true }` (behind
`BackForwardCacheForComponentExtensionFrames`) now allocate isolated
`WindowAgent` instances per `BrowsingInstance` and bypass the
`kExtensionFrame` BFCache block, preserving extension frame state
across back/forward navigations.

While an extension frame is in BackForwardCache,
`NativeExtensionBindingsSystem` drops incoming extension API events
targeted at that frame (excluding content scripts) so cached frames do
not execute background listeners while frozen. Support is initially
restricted to component extensions while the architecture is validated
in production.

Fixed: 543666778

6f6aaea7104b4d69d84251c9d1fc0d7a02530eaa [ diff ] 
[Tab] Fix stale comments and assert content-state invariants in TabImpl

Several TabImpl comments describe code that no longer exists
(showInternal(), subclasses, updateAttachment()) or are wrong (the
deferred ContentView is a stub, not null; getWindowAndroid() can be
null). "Frozen" is also overloaded: Tab#isFrozen() is unrelated to
NativePage#isFrozen(), so its contract is now spelled out. The
checkAttachedToActivity() ternary is rewritten as an if/return so the
corrected comment maps onto each branch; evaluation order is unchanged.

The new asserts document invariants every caller already satisfies:
lazy-load params and restored TabState are never both passed to
initialize(), and WebContentsState is always cleared before live
WebContents are installed. They let later refactors rely on these states
being mutually exclusive. No behavior change.

Bug: None

tree
84daa27fc8503b01f8c3a607d2164c2c2244a1b2
parent
dc28bbc3a83e8fc08e1d933831bdc65dcd04dd62 [ diff ] 
[iOS] Migrate profile unit tests to AddManagedProfileWithBuilder

Update profile unit tests in ios/chrome/browser/intelligence/bwg/first_run.

In Chrome on iOS, there is only one personal profile and any additional
profile must be a managed profile associated with a managed identity via
AccountProfileMapper.

This CL is in preparation for https://crrev.com/c/8579025, which removes
TestProfileIOS::Builder::SetName() and enforces that
TestProfileManagerIOS::AddProfileWithBuilder() is only used once to
create the personal profile:
- Use AddManagedProfileWithBuilder() to create managed profiles instead
  of calling AddProfileWithBuilder() multiple times with custom names.
- Remove unnecessary TestProfileIOS::Builder::SetName() calls and
  hardcoded default profile names.
- Rename profile variables to make the distinction between the personal
  profile and managed profiles explicit.

This CL was uploaded by git cl split.

tree
59fa57381dcfa89dc205cf06c0712edf35b3fc19
parent
5caceed13ca97820e5426c86bc383aa3d6ab28f7 [ diff ] 
[ios] Restrict top inset corner adaptation to windowed mode

On iOS 26+, safeAreaLayoutRegionWithCornerAdaptation calculates corner
adaptation insets for display curvature. While intended for iPad Stage
Manager window controls, this was queried unconditionally on all
devices. On iPhones in landscape, this returned a non-zero top inset
despite rootSafeAreaInsets.top being zero, creating empty space at the
top of the web page when the omnibox is at the bottom in Chrome Next
IA.

Restrict topInsetWithCornerAdaptation to when the window is in
windowed mode.

[iOS] Remove expired flag composebox-fetch-contextual-suggestions-for-multiple-attachments

The flag has expired and been removed from chrome://flags per
//docs/flag_expiry.md. This CL removes the flag entry, its metadata,
descriptions, and the kComposeboxFetchContextualSuggestionsForMultipleAttachments
feature, which was disabled by default.

ComposeboxInputPlateMediator's -reloadSuggestions keeps the default
(disabled) behavior: autocomplete is restarted only when there is at most
one attachment. A unit test is added to cover this behavior.

45cd1c3637f86ce6bd05fe764394af313ec2fcb4
parent
1fbbc6c22fc1f4e4493440cce0c8ab24120f17c8 [ diff ] 
Revert "[clang] Fix O(n^2) scan in FilteredASTConsumer system-header skip"

This reverts commit 4f7ec02084981506810612b8a27d0ab556239f2d.

The added assert() doesn't seem to be true, see:

Reason for revert: https://issues.chromium.org/issues/573948961

Failure Link: eg https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/ToTAndroid/39053/overview

Original change's description:
> [clang] Fix O(n^2) scan in FilteredASTConsumer system-header skip
>
> SkipSystemHeaders() in FilteredASTConsumer keeps declarations that a
> non-system macro expands into, even when the declaration otherwise
> lives entirely in a system header. It does this by recording every
> macro-expansion location in locations_ and, for each system-header
> top-level declaration, linearly scanning locations_ in
> HasNonSystemCodeIn(range) to check for a match.
>
> locations_ is almost always empty, so this is normally free. But on a
> translation unit that expands many non-system macros into system
> headers, both locations_ and the number of system-header top-level
> declarations grow together, making the scan quadratic. This was
> observed to turn one translation unit's compile time from ~30s to
> ~60 minutes purely from enabling a plugin that uses this consumer.
>
> locations_ is only appended to by MacroExpands(), which fires in
> preprocessing order, so it is already sorted by
> isBeforeInTranslationUnit(). HasNonSystemCodeIn() can binary-search it
> with std::lower_bound instead, turning the per-declaration cost from
> O(locations_) into O(log locations_). An assert at insertion time
> validates the sorted invariant in asserts builds.
>
> Bug: 569927804
> Change-Id: Ib03f058d5f997d6d9db5716e265eae7fbedf2979

[FSA] Await file.remove() in ObserveFailsWhenFileDoesNotExist

3def9d060ed4d10f6422a3f124e57f73c0e985f1
[ log ]

tree
dc9b031666508afdc8a1a11b273247a77c06e4c1
parent
800e0f545b5f0437a7a8203778faae756ab6b745 [ diff ] 
[FSA] Await file.remove() in ObserveFailsWhenFileDoesNotExist

In FileSystemAccessObserverBrowserTest.ObserveFailsWhenFileDoesNotExist,
file.remove() was called without await immediately before calling
observer.observe(file).

On Windows, when LocalFileUtil::DeleteFile (::DeleteFileW) runs
concurrently with the symlink/junction check in
FileSystemAccessObserverHost::DidResolveTransferTokenToObserve
(base::NormalizeFilePath), ::DeleteFileW can POSIX-unlink the file while
NormalizeFilePath holds a FILE_SHARE_DELETE handle.
::GetFinalPathNameByHandle then returns the \\ path, causing check_path
!= path to evaluate to true and failing with
FILE_ERROR_INVALID_OPERATION (InvalidModificationError) instead of
NotFoundError.

Await file.remove() so the file is removed before observer.observe(file)
is called, matching ObserveFailsWhenDirectoryDoesNotExist.
